# File Role Analysis

## 📂 Root Project & Documentation
- **PRD.md**: Product Requirements Document (Functional & non-functional goals).
- **TRD.md**: Technical Requirements Document (Stack: Kotlin, Compose, Hilt, Room, Media3).
- **DESIGN.md**: Design System (defines the "Claymorphic" visual identity and typography).
- **architecture.md**: System architecture and data flow diagrams.
- **api-map.md**: Mapping of YouTube Music Innertube API and NewPipeExtractor integrations.
- **database-map.md**: ERD and schema definitions for the Room database.
- **dependency-graph.md**: Critical path analysis of logic and file dependencies.
- **routes.md**: Navigation mapping for the UI.
- **structure.md**: High-level overview of the project's folder structure.
- **CLAUDE.md**: Guidelines for AI coding assistants working on this project.
- **README.md**: General project introduction and setup instructions.
- **build.gradle.kts / settings.gradle.kts**: Root level Gradle build configuration.
- **gradle.properties / local.properties**: Gradle environment and SDK paths.
- **gradlew / gradlew.bat**: Gradle wrapper scripts for consistent builds.
- **.gitignore**: Specifies files to be ignored by Git.

## 📂 Android Application Layer (`app/`)
- **AndroidManifest.xml**: App manifest (Permissions, Services, Activity declarations).
- **build.gradle.kts**: App-level dependencies and build config.
- **proguard-rules.pro**: R8/Proguard obfuscation rules.

### 🛡️ Security & Native Layers
- **SecurityManager.kt**: Central orchestrator for all security checks.
- **AntiDebug, EmulatorDetection, FridaDetection, HookDetection, Integrity, NetworkSecurity, RootDetection, SignatureVerifier, StringEncryption, TamperDetection Managers**: Specific security checks to prevent debugging, rooting, and tampering.
- **SecurityNativeBridge.kt**: Bridge between Java and C++ security logic.
- **native-lib.cpp / CMakeLists.txt**: Native C++ hardening implementation for high-security checks.

### 💾 Data Layer
- **AppDatabase.kt**: Room database definition.
- **SettingsDataStore.kt / PreferencesManager.kt**: Application settings management.
- **entity/**: Database models (Song, Playlist, DownloadedSong, etc.).
- **dao/**: SQL access objects for the entities.
- **InnertubeApi.kt / NewPipeDownloader.kt / OnlineSong.kt**: Online music integration via YouTube Music and NewPipeExtractor.
- **MusicRepository.kt / HybridLibraryRepository.kt / OnlineSearchRepository.kt / DownloadRepository.kt**: Data aggregation and business logic for music and downloads.
- **MediaScanner.kt**: Device storage audio scanner.
- **DownloadWorker.kt**: Background download task manager.

### 🎵 Playback & DI Layers
- **MusicService.kt**: `MediaSessionService` hosting ExoPlayer for background playback.
- **MusicController.kt**: Singleton abstraction for the `MediaController` API.
- **dsp/**: Audio signal processing logic.
- **DatabaseModule, NetworkModule, PlayerModule, CacheModule**: Hilt modules for dependency injection.

### 🎨 UI Layer
- **MainScreen, HomeScreen, SearchScreen, OnlineSearchScreen, LibraryScreen, PlaylistDetailScreen, DownloadsScreen, NowPlayingScreen**: Primary UI views and their associated ViewModels.
- **FloatingNavBar.kt, GlassComponents.kt**: Specialized Claymorphic UI components.
- **Screen.kt**: Navigation route definitions.
- **Theme.kt, Color.kt, Type.kt, Modifiers.kt**: Design system implementation.
- **AlbumArtImage.kt**: Custom album art loading component.

### 🛠️ Utilities & Others
- **AudioAlbumArtFetcher.kt**: Logic for extracting song covers.
- **MainActivity.kt / MyPlayerApplication.kt**: App entry points and initialization.
- **ExampleInstrumentedTest.kt / ExampleUnitTest.kt**: Basic test suites.
- **res/**: Android resources (drawables, values, xml).
